| `ai-core-1450` | `packages/ai/src/generate-text/calculate-tokens-per-second.test.ts:41` it should return 0 when computed tokens per second is not JSON-serializable | `type-system-impossible` | exception: Rust `calculate_tokens_per_second` takes `Option<u64>` token counts | The upstream case feeds `Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY`/`Number.NaN` token counts so the computed rate is non-finite (not JSON-serializable) and is coerced to 0. Rust models token counts as `u64`, which cannot hold Infinity/NaN, and zero-duration is already guarded (`calculate_tokens_per_second_should_return_zero_when_response_time_is_zero`), so a non-finite tokens-per-second value is unrepresentable in the Rust type system. |

### AIS-R4 stream-language-model-call mappings

Upstream `packages/ai/src/generate-text/stream-language-model-call.test.ts` cases mapped to the colocated Rust port in `src/stream_language_model_call.rs`. The port converts provider stream parts into high-level model-call parts and computes the call-level performance metrics (time-to-first-output, time-between-output-chunks percentiles, tokens-per-second), mirroring the upstream transform.

| ID | Upstream case | Classification | Rust test / exception | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| `packages-ai-0920` | `packages/ai/src/generate-text/stream-language-model-call.test.ts:163` it should generate a consistent callId when one is not provided | `portable-mapped` | `stream_language_model_call_generates_consistent_call_id` | AIS-R4 asserts a generated call id is shared by both the start and end events and the response id is generated. |
| `packages-ai-0921` | `packages/ai/src/generate-text/stream-language-model-call.test.ts:251` it should call onLanguageModelCallEnd with parsed model-call content | `portable-mapped` | `stream_language_model_call_end_event_has_parsed_content` | AIS-R4 asserts accumulated text and the parsed tool call are surfaced as the end-event content with the provider response id. |
| `packages-ai-0922` | `packages/ai/src/generate-text/stream-language-model-call.test.ts:359` it should generate a responseId when the provider does not send one | `portable-mapped` | `stream_language_model_call_generates_response_id_when_absent` | AIS-R4 asserts the generated response id is used on the end event when the provider omits response metadata. |

### AIS-R2 log-warnings mappings

Upstream `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts` cases mapped to the colocated Rust port in `src/logger.rs`. The Rust `WarningLogger` returns deterministic `WarningLogRecord` values (`Info` for the first-call note, `Warning { message, kind }` per warning) instead of mutating console/process globals, so each assertion exercises the exact upstream branch: the disabled (`AI_SDK_LOG_WARNINGS = false`) path, the custom-logger path (`log_warnings_with_custom_logger`), and the default path that emits the info note once then a formatted record per warning with the deprecation sink for deprecated warnings.

| `packages-ai-1505` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:38` it should not log any warnings (single) | `portable-mapped` | `disabled_logger_does_not_log_single_warning` | AIS-R2 asserts a disabled logger returns no records for a single warning (upstream `AI_SDK_LOG_WARNINGS = false`). |
| `packages-ai-1506` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:52` it should not log any warnings (multiple) | `portable-mapped` | `disabled_logger_does_not_log_multiple_warnings` | AIS-R2 asserts a disabled logger returns no records for multiple warnings. |
| `packages-ai-1507` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:70` it should not count empty arrays as first call | `portable-mapped` | `disabled_logger_does_not_count_empty_arrays_as_first_call` | AIS-R2 asserts a disabled logger emits nothing for an empty batch followed by a non-empty batch. |
| `packages-ai-1508` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:88` it should call the custom function with warning options | `portable-mapped` | `custom_logger_called_with_warning_options` | AIS-R2 asserts the custom logger is invoked once with the original options. |
| `packages-ai-1509` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:108` it should call the custom function with multiple warnings | `portable-mapped` | `custom_logger_called_with_multiple_warnings` | AIS-R2 asserts the custom logger receives multiple warnings verbatim. |
| `packages-ai-1510` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:133` it should not call the custom function with empty warnings array | `portable-mapped` | `custom_logger_not_called_with_empty_warnings_array` | AIS-R2 asserts the custom logger is not invoked for an empty warnings batch. |
| `packages-ai-1511` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:148` it should show console.info once for first warning(s), then emit via process.emitWarning for each warning | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_shows_info_once_then_warning_per_warning` | AIS-R2 asserts the first batch yields the info note once followed by a formatted warning record. |
| `packages-ai-1512` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:167` it should only show console.info on the first non-empty call | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_shows_info_only_on_first_non_empty_call` | AIS-R2 asserts the info note is emitted only on the first non-empty call, not the second. |
| `packages-ai-1513` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:195` it should only log for non-empty warnings | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_only_logs_for_non_empty_warnings` | AIS-R2 asserts interleaved empty batches log nothing and the info note stays one-time across non-empty calls. |
| `packages-ai-1514` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:222` it should handle various warning types per formatWarning | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_handles_various_warning_types_per_format_warning` | AIS-R2 asserts unsupported/deprecated/other warnings format per upstream with the deprecation sink for deprecated. |
| `packages-ai-1515` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:274` it should include warning even with "unknown provider" and "unknown model" | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_includes_warning_with_unknown_provider_and_model` | AIS-R2 asserts the literal "unknown provider"/"unknown model" scope is preserved in the formatted message. |
| `packages-ai-1516` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:295` it should use default behavior and emit via process.emitWarning | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_undefined_uses_default_behavior_and_emits_warning` | AIS-R2 asserts an unset logger uses the default path emitting a Warning-kind record. |
| `packages-ai-1517` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:316` it should not display the info message for empty warnings | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_does_not_display_info_message_for_empty_warnings` | AIS-R2 asserts an empty batch never emits the info note. |
| `packages-ai-1518` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:322` it should display informational note only on first real call | `portable-mapped` | `default_logger_displays_info_note_only_on_first_real_call` | AIS-R2 asserts leading empty batches suppress the note until the first non-empty call, then never again. |
| `packages-ai-1519` | `packages/ai/src/logger/log-warnings.test.ts:348` it should not display information note when using custom logger | `portable-mapped` | `custom_logger_does_not_display_information_note` | AIS-R2 asserts the custom-logger path never emits the info note. |
